# LUFFY — Product, Behaviour & GUI Tracker

Version: 1.31-att03-owner-scope-sol2

Canonical source: `LUFFY_Product_Tracker_v1.yaml`. Primary view: read-only Dashboard Tracker. Markdown is optional; XLSX is deprecated/non-authoritative history.

GOV-07: **DEFERRED / NOT_REQUIRED_FOR_CURRENT_RELEASE**. Deployment/runtime identity remains unproven and is required before future deployment/live activation claims. OWN-03 is CLOSED at architecture/offline scope. OWN-04 is BLOCKED after verified display fixes on MON-01/MON-05 dependency evidence; ACC-03 is the next independent GUI evidence recommendation, unselected. NEXT retains the existing CLOSED OBS-05 terminal slot for reader compatibility.

## Status definitions

**EVIDENCE_TO_MAP**: Existing requirement recorded; exact row-level evidence has not yet been linked. This is NOT a finding that the feature is missing, broken or reopened.

**OPEN**: A specific measured gap or needed proof is recorded; work is not automatically authorized.

**IN_PROGRESS**: The single currently authorized work package. Result pending; do not launch parallel packages.

**AWAITING_EVIDENCE**: Implementation or a fix may exist; the specified operational/reproduction/closure evidence is pending.

**AWAITING_OWNER**: A visual approval, scope/policy value or adoption decision is needed. Not permission to invent it.

**CLOSED**: Only this row's exact closing condition is reported satisfied at its recorded revision/scope. Later regressions need a new evidence-linked event.

**DEFERRED**: Outside current release scope by design; visible but not counted as a current mandatory defect.

**BLOCKED**: Cannot proceed under its contract; an exact dependency, external condition or safety fact must be recorded.

## Operating rules

**Authority**: SDD.md defines the target. STATE/NEXT remain the current summary and single selected work package. This is their detailed derived ledger, not another architecture authority.

**One data set**: Maintain this YAML as canonical detailed data. Dashboard Tracker reads validated YAML and NEXT. Markdown is optional generated reference. Historical XLSX is deprecated/non-authoritative and excluded from synchronization and adoption checks.

**Current work**: OWN-04 CLOSED for current architecture/offline GUI scope. MON-01/MON-05 are deferred current-phase obligations, mandatory and unproven before future activation. GUI-01 eligible for functional evidence work; ACC-03 unresolved. SEC-01/RUN-01/GOV-07 remain deferred. No activation authorized.

**Activation**: No item, green cell, spreadsheet formula or passing test activates trading, paid providers, new workers, a restart or a scope expansion.

**Intake**: A new finding needs ID, parent requirement, why, source or exact violated invariant, evidence/reproducer, impact, dependencies and closure condition. Unknown origin stays unknown.

**No moving goalposts**: Keep the acceptance version that the work started under. Change it only with an explicit reason/owner decision. A different requirement is a linked item, not a silent edit.

**Closure**: Link exact test/run/config/code identity; verify normal caller, relevant failure/restart cases and touched frozen invariants. Mark only the demonstrated proof level.

**Historical PASS**: Do not convert a package-level count into per-behaviour or runtime proof. Do not rerun old audits solely because an item is EVIDENCE_TO_MAP.

**Regression**: Preserve prior closure. Record a new event with reproducer, affected invariant and evidence for introducing revision; repair only the justified scope.

**GUI**: Track per-screen functional proof, runtime truth and owner visual approval separately. No agent may award the owner's visual approval.

**Blocked work**: Record internal dependency, policy, external evidence or owner gate distinctly. Do not simulate missing authority or fabricate retrospective evidence to close it.

**Observations**: Warm-up, no opportunity, no eligible rule, disabled paid provider and policy-blocked research can be valid outcomes. They cannot prove an unexercised capability.

**Metrics**: Summary counts are tracker workflow counts, NOT percent complete, weighted product progress or readiness. No fixed profitability/sample/retention thresholds are invented.

**Updates**: After each completed package: attach result, update affected IDs, add new issues, record history and select exactly one approved next ID in NEXT.

**Data**: Identical immutable payloads may share storage. Distinct observation times/identities remain. Backups are intentional redundancy; retention/deletion requires a proved dependency boundary.

**Historical observation limit**: Retained observed runtime revision is 9385f792…; latest corrected-harness attempt stopped at 240.033s with first cycle 259.776s and zero completed window cycles. Repository-control commits after that are documentation/tracker changes and must not be represented as observed runtime.

**Adoption**: Owner accepted representation and the read-only Tracker navigation. OPEN-09 closes through linked SDD/STATE/NEXT and explicit engineering ownership of YAML/optional Markdown synchronization. Product-wide EVIDENCE_TO_MAP does not prevent adoption.

**Safety exception**: An immediate observed safety incident may require containment under existing authority; record the issue and outcome promptly. A tracker queue never overrides safety.

## Readiness gates

### M0 — One current project plan

Current: Tracker adopted as detailed evidence ledger; owner representation accepted; STATE/NEXT reconciliation selects one diagnosis-only next item. Row-level EVIDENCE_TO_MAP remains normal mapping work, not a blocker to using the tracker.

Close when: One accepted tracker linked to SDD/STATE/NEXT; existing evidence mapped; one authorized item. No contradictory current status.

Items: GOV-01, GOV-02, GOV-03, GOV-04, GOV-06, OPEN-09

Limit: Not a new architecture review.

### M1 — Stable controlled observation

Current: Latest corrected-harness attempt blocked: first cycle 259.776s > 240s, zero completed window cycles. PERF-01 is selected for diagnosis before any code change.

Close when: Approved instrumented FROZEN window completes on fixed code with correct startup/shutdown, unchanged safety boundaries and measured all-store growth.

Items: RUN-01, RUN-02, RUN-03, MON-01, MON-02, MON-03, ATT-02, DATA-12, LLM-02, WORK-01

Limit: Does not establish research, profitable edge or real-money readiness.

### M2 — Supported autonomous research

Current: Build claims and selected reproductions exist; enabled worker workload and prospective result not established.

Close when: Normal event→question→source→Bank→experiment/referee path is observed under approved data/resource/provider scope, including legitimate negative/inconclusive results.

Items: WRLD-03, WRLD-05, ATT-01, RES-01, RES-02, RES-05, RES-08, RES-09, QNT-08, OPEN-01, OPEN-02, OPEN-03

Limit: No forced questions or silent gate enablement.

### M3 — Governed strategy/portfolio/outcome loop

Current: Fixture/build evidence reported; real supported rule/economics/account evidence and observation remain partial.

Close when: An authorized supported path connects exact strategy/context/Portfolio/Risk/action/outcome and bounded later consumer, with correct refusal branches.

Items: STR-01, STR-02, STR-03, PORT-01, PORT-02, PORT-08, RISK-01, EXE-01, OUT-01, OUT-03, LRN-04

Limit: Test/demo evidence stays labelled; empty registries are policy/evidence blockers.

### M4 — Owner-usable GUI

Current: Stage8 broad historical tests reported; current per-screen map and owner approvals pending.

Close when: Nine screens have required functional/runtime evidence and exact owner visual approval; shared interaction/safety/performance checks pass.

Items: GUI-01, GUI-02, GUI-03, GUI-04, GUI-05, GUI-06, GUI-07, GUI-08, GUI-09, GUX-01, GUX-11

Limit: Do not infer all GUI approval from an approved screenshot or API test.

### M5 — Explicit real-money release

Current: NOT_AUTHORIZED; current operation restricted to FROZEN demo observation.

Close when: Owner-authorized exact versions satisfy actual account/venue/probation, cost/capacity, safety and release requirements.

Items: REL-01, STR-03, RISK-03, EXE-05, EXE-06, SEC-01, OWN-02

Limit: No automatic activation from any prior gate.

### M6 — Economic usefulness and disciplined expansion

Current: Not established; no profitability guarantee or arbitrary quota.

Close when: Owner-defined evaluation establishes actual net economics, uncertainty and operating cost; expansion earns its complexity through approved evidence.

Items: GOV-10, ACC-03, REL-02, REL-03, REL-04

Limit: An honest negative result is evidence, not proof of profitable completion.
